- **splatter-scrna-seq-simulation**: Use when benchmarking a scRNA-seq data generation method (e.g. scGAN) against Splatter on the same real training cells, evaluating with MMD and random-forest discrimination AUC (RF-AUC). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/splatter-scrna-seq-simulation))

- **splatter-single-cell-rnaseq-trajectory-simulation**: Use when benchmarking feature selection or trajectory inference methods on scRNA-seq data where ground-truth cluster labels, DE genes, and latent step pseudotime must be known and noise must be systematically varied via BCV. - **splice-aware-rna-read-alignment**: Use when raw nanopore fast5 direct RNA reads have been basecalled to FASTA and must be mapped to a genome (e.g. GRCh38 or TAIR10) for downstream per-site m6A prediction with mAFiA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/splice-aware-rna-seq-read-alignment))
